### what the compressed phase is worth, in fp32 steps (polish fp32)

k_eq = steps fp32 needs from scratch to reach the hybrid's accuracy; head start = k_eq - k (inf: the hybrid beats fp32 at any step count <= T).

### what the compressed phase is worth, in fp32 steps (polish fp32)

k_eq = steps fp32 needs from scratch to reach the hybrid's accuracy; head start = k_eq - k (inf: the hybrid beats fp32 at any step count <= T).

| cell | c127+f1: acc / k_eq / head start | c126+f2: acc / k_eq / head start | c124+f4: acc / k_eq / head start | c120+f8: acc / k_eq / head start | c112+f16: acc / k_eq / head start | c96+f32: acc / k_eq / head start |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### memory and compute per configuration (linear weights; embeddings excluded)

bits/weight = stored bits per linear weight; extra = polish bits beyond the compressed model; traffic = weight bits read per inference relative to 16 steps of the compressed model (every step reads every weight; low-rank polish adds r(in+out) fp16 values per matrix).
